1. **Infinite Loop on EINTR**: The loop continues reading until `bytes` is non-zero or there's no error, but if the file is in an EINTR state (e.g., during a kernel interruptible loop), `ferror` might not accurately reflect the error condition, potentially causing the loop to hang.
